## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```

  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. On this Mac, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos, or tidy up a folder of files, while you keep working on something else.
```


### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信方式で、UDPは保証せず速さを優先する方式です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día tu rama o limpiar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, porque deja el historial lineal.
Usa merge cuando la rama ya está publicada o la usan otros, porque no reescribe commits y conserva el historial tal cual pasó.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接、保证顺序的字节流传输；UDP 是不可靠的、无连接的数据报传输，只管发，不保证到达和顺序，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared feature branch to tidy it up and keep history linear before opening a PR. Merge when the branch is already shared with others, or when you want the record of parallel work kept as it happened.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records each change to a database before it is applied to the main data files, so the database can replay the log and recover committed work after a crash. Writing to the log sequentially is fast and durable, and the actual data pages can be updated later, at checkpoints.
```

```
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五京沪，600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">班次（按价格）</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<script>
var LIMIT = 600;
function render(v) {
  var f = v || {};
  var rows = (f.rows || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });
  var under = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.price < LIMIT; });
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  if (!rows.length) { cnt.textContent = '—'; cnt.className = 'k-sum-value k-none'; cnt.textContent = '还没记'; sub.textContent = '每天早上更新'; return; }
  cnt.className = 'k-sum-value';
  cnt.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
  var low = under.length ? '最低 ¥' + under[0].price : '暂无';
  sub.textContent = low + ' · 第 ' + (f.updated || '') + ' 次更新';
  list.innerHTML = '';
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = r.train;
    var sb = document.createElement('div');
    sb.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sb.textContent = r.left || '';
    main.appendChild(sb);
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.style.color = r.price < LIMIT ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
    val.textContent = '¥' + r.price;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
var got = ducter.get('fares');
if (got && typeof got.then === 'function') { got.then(function (x) { render(x && x.rows ? x : x); }); }
else { render(got && got.rows ? got : got); }
ducter.onChange(function () { var g = ducter.get('fares'); if (g && typeof g.then === 'function') g.then(render); else render(g); });
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"fares":{"day":"周五","updated":"第 3 天","rows":[{"train":"G1","price":553,"left":"二等座余 12 张"},{"train":"G3","price":553,"left":"二等座余 4 张"},{"train":"G7","price":626,"left":""}]}}
```

```
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">writes/s · p99 · compaction stalls</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nightly runs</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;","\"":"&quot;"}[c];});}
function render(d){
  var nights=(d&&d.nights)||[];
  var list=document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML='';
  if(!nights.length){
    list.innerHTML='<div class="k-empty">Nothing yet. Each night’s run lands here.</div>';
    document.getElementById('latest').innerHTML='<span class="k-none">Not yet</span>';
    return;
  }
  nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function(n){
    var row=document.createElement('div');
    row.className='k-row';
    row.innerHTML='<div class="k-row-main">'+esc(n.night)+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">p99 '+esc(n.p99)+' · stalls '+esc(n.stalls)+'</div><div class="k-row-value">'+esc(n.writes)+'</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  var last=nights[nights.length-1];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent=last.writes;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent=last.night+' · p99 '+last.p99+' · stalls '+last.stalls;
}
ducter.onChange(render);
ducter.get('nights').then ? null : null;
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(function(v){ render({nights:v}); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k/s","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k/s","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}
```

```
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const sum = xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = "¥" + sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "还没记";
  const ul = document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-value"></div>';
    row.querySelector(".k-row-main").textContent = x.what;
    row.querySelector(".k-row-sub") ? null : row.querySelector(".k-row-main").insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", '<div class="k-row-sub"></div>');
    row.querySelector(".k-row-sub").textContent = x.day;
    row.querySelector(".k-row-value").textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    ul.appendChild(row);
  }
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick = () => {
  const what = document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan = Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day = document.getElementById("day").value.trim();
  if (!what || !yuan) return;
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  xs.push({what, yuan, day});
  ducter.set("expenses", xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value = "";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value = "";
  document.getElementById("day").value = "";
};
ducter.summary("合计 ¥1,966");
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>
```

```
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>

    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tick each one as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-app{font-family:-apple-system,system-ui,sans-serif;background:var(--d-bg);color:var(--d-ink);padding:16px;max-width:360px;margin:0 auto}
  .k-sum{background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px;margin-bottom:16px}
  .k-sum-label{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted)}
  .k-sum-value{font-size:32px;font-weight:600;margin:2px 0}
  .k-sum-sub{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-soft)}
  .k-h{font-size:15px;font-weight:600;margin:0 0 8px}
  .k-list{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px}
  .k-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;cursor:pointer;text-align:left;width:100%;color:inherit;font:inherit}
  .k-tick{flex:none;width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:7px;border:1.5px solid var(--d-muted);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-conten … ion render(){
    const waiting = ducter.get('waiting') || DEF;
    const done = ducter.get('done') || {};
    const list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    let left = 0;
    waiting.forEach(it => {
      const on = !!done[it.id];
      if(!on) left++;
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.className = 'k-row';
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on));
      b.innerHTML = '<span class="k-tick">' + (on ? '✓' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span class="k-row-main"><span class="k-row-title"></span><div class="k-row-sub"></div></span>';
      b.querySelector('.k-row-title').textContent = it.title;
      b.querySelector('.k-row-sub').textContent = it.who;
      b.onclick = () => {
        const d = Object.assign({}, ducter.get('done') || {});
        d[it.id] = !d[it.id];
        ducter.set('done', d);
      };
      list.appendChild(b);
    });
    document.getElementById('left').textContent = left;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = left === 0 ? 'all landed' : 'tick each one as it lands';
    ducter.summary(left + ' waiting, ' + (waiting.length - left) + ' landed');
  }
```

```
<script>
let cat='交通', editIdx=null;

const fmt=n=>'¥'+Number(n).toLocaleString('zh-CN',{maximumFracti … ssName='k-row-main';main.textContent=x.what||'未命名';
    const s=document.createElement('div');s.className='k-row-sub';s.textContent=[x.trip,x.day,x.cat].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');

    row.append(main,v);row.onclick=()=>edit(i);

function save(){
  const amt=$('amt').value.trim();if(!amt){$('amt').focus();return}
  const obj={what:$('what').value.trim()||'未命名',trip:$('trip').value.trim(),day:$('day').value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:Number(amt)};
  const xs=ducter.get('items')||[];
  if(editIdx!==null)xs[editIdx]=obj;else xs.push(obj);
  ducter.set('items',xs);clearForm();$('add').open=false;draw();
}
function del(){
  if(editIdx===null)return;
  const xs=ducter.get('items')||[];xs.splice(editIdx,1);
  ducter.set('items',xs);clearForm();$('add').open=false;draw();
}
document.querySelectorAll('#cats .k-chip').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setCat(b.dataset.cat));
$('save').onclick=save;$('del').onclick=del;
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
```

```
要求：在 ~/dev/foo 的 TTL 测试里，补一条覆盖"正好到期那一刻"的用例，使用现有的假时钟。具体验证：时间推进到恰好等于 TTL 的那一刻，该值应视为已过期（或按现有语义的边界行为，以代码里的约定为准，并在报告里写明你采用的是哪种边界）；到期前一毫秒（或最小单位）仍有效。

完成标准：新测试通过，全部 TTL 相关测试通过；报告里给出测试输出和你对边界语义的判断依据。

范围外：不要合并到主分支，不要推送，不要开 PR。改动留在现有分支上提交即可。
```
